href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#image_skyline\"></span>\n\n<p id=\"mwCQ\"><b id=\"mwCg\">Souk Ahras</b> (<a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language\" title=\"Arabic language\" about=\"#mwt12\" typeof=\"mw:Transclusion\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwCw\" data-mw=\"{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;langx&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Langx&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ar&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ولاية سوق أهراس&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}\">Arabic</a><span about=\"#mwt12\">: </span><span lang=\"ar\" dir=\"rtl\" about=\"#mwt12\">ولاية سوق أهراس</span><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text\" about=\"#mwt12\" id=\"mwDA\">) is a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Provinces_of_Algeria\" title=\"Provinces of Algeria\" id=\"mwDQ\">province</a> (<i id=\"mwDg\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wilaya\" title=\"Wilaya\" id=\"mwDw\">wilaya</a></i>) in the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aures\" title=\"Aures\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwEA\">Aures</a> region in <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algeria\" title=\"Algeria\" id=\"mwEQ\">Algeria</a>, named after its capital, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Souk_Ahras\" title=\"Souk Ahras\" id=\"mwEg\">Souk Ahras</a>. It stands on the border between Algeria and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tunisia\" title=\"Tunisia\" id=\"mwEw\">Tunisia</a>, and as of 2008, Souk Ahras had a population of 440,299 people.</p>\n\n<meta property=\"mw:PageProp/toc\" id=\"mwFA\" data-mw=\"{&quot;autoGenerated&quot;:true}\"></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"1\" id=\"mwFQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Geography\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Geography\">Geography</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1\" title=\"Edit section: Geography\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<figure class=\"mw-halign-left\" typeof=\"mw:File/Thumb\" id=\"mwFg\"><a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Souk_ahras_topography.jpg\" class=\"mw-file-description\" id=\"mwFw\"><img resource=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Souk_ahras_topography.jpg\" src=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/be/Souk_ahras_topography.jpg/250px-Souk_ahras_topography.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail\" decoding=\"async\" srcset=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/be/Souk_ahras_topography.jpg/500px-Souk_ahras_topography.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x\" data-file-width=\"2221\" data-file-height=\"1371\" data-file-type=\"bitmap\" height=\"148\" width=\"240\" class=\"mw-file-element\" id=\"mwGA\"></a><figcaption id=\"mwGQ\">Souk ahras topography</figcaption></figure>\n<p id=\"mwGg\">Souk Ahras is situated in the extreme north east of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algeria\" title=\"Algeria\" id=\"mwGw\">Algeria</a>. Its area is 4360<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwHA\">&nbsp;</span>km<sup id=\"mwHQ\">2</sup>.\nIts border to the north is the province of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El_Taref\" title=\"El Taref\" id=\"mwHg\">El Taref</a>; in the east is <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tunisia\" title=\"Tunisia\" id=\"mwHw\">Tunisia</a>; westward is the province of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guelma\" title=\"Guelma\" id=\"mwIA\">Guelma</a> and the province of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oum_el_Bouaghi\" title=\"Oum el Bouaghi\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwIQ\">Oum el Bouaghi</a>; in the south the province of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tébessa\" title=\"Tébessa\" id=\"mwIg\">Tebessa</a>. Its geography is composed of three distinct areas, with a mountainous north, the central region being lowlands, and the south being fertile.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"2\" id=\"mwIw\" aria-labelledby=\"Climate\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Climate\">Climate</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2\" title=\"Edit section: Climate\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwJA\">The city of Souk Ahras has a semi continental and humid climate, heavy rains in the north in winter and very hot and dry in the south during summer. The rains are 350–750<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwJQ\">&nbsp;</span>mm/year and the temperatures vary from 1<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwJg\">&nbsp;</span>°C to 14<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwJw\">&nbsp;</span>°C in winter and from 25<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwKA\">&nbsp;</span>°C to 38<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwKQ\">&nbsp;</span>°C in summer.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwKg\">The fauna is very rich in Souk Ahras, where species such as the hare, partridge, fox, sparrow, ferret, porcupine and barbarian hart can be found. The flora as well is very rich, with species such as:</p>\n<ul id=\"mwKw\"><li id=\"mwLA\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quercus_suber\" title=\"Quercus suber\" id=\"mwLQ\">Cork oak</a>: 12,000 hectares</li>\n<li id=\"mwLg\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quercus_tlemcenensis\" title=\"Quercus tlemcenensis\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwLw\">Zeen oak</a>: 4,600 hectares</li>\n<li id=\"mwMA\">Ash-tree</li>\n<li id=\"mwMQ\">Flowering ash</li>\n<li id=\"mwMg\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pinus_halepensis\" title=\"Pinus halepensis\" id=\"mwMw\">Aleppo pine (Pinus halepensis)</a></li>\n<li id=\"mwNA\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tetraclinis\" title=\"Tetraclinis\" id=\"mwNQ\">Cypress tree</a></li>\n<li id=\"mwNg\">Mastic-tree</li>\n<li id=\"mwNw\">Lavender</li>\n<li id=\"mwOA\">Heather</li></ul>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"3\" id=\"mwOQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Trading_activities\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Trading_activities\">Trading activities</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3\" title=\"Edit section: Trading activities\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwOg\">Trading activities are widespread in Sedrata and Zaarouria were many public and private companies have been set up:</p>\n<ul id=\"mwOw\"><li id=\"mwPA\">National company of painting</li>\n<li id=\"mwPQ\">National company of textile</li>\n<li id=\"mwPg\">Brick-making company</li>\n<li id=\"mwPw\">Sedrata metallic company</li>\n<li id=\"mwQA\">Mansouri paper factory</li>\n<li id=\"mwQQ\">Hamada dairy</li>\n<li id=\"mwQg\">ALKALAM factory</li></ul>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"4\" id=\"mwQw\" aria-labelledby=\"Agriculture\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Agriculture\">Agriculture</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4\" title=\"Edit section: Agriculture\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwRA\">The most important activities in this region are: agriculture, breeding and com cultivation. The total area is about 436,000 hectares. 235,000 hectares are consecrated to agriculture.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"5\" id=\"mwRQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Infrastructure\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Infrastructure\">Infrastructure</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5\" title=\"Edit section: Infrastructure\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwRg\">Basic under structures and the railway system:</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwRw\">Main roads: 600<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwSA\">&nbsp;</span>km\nSecondary roads: 1600<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwSQ\">&nbsp;</span>km.\nThe railway from Souk Ahras to Annaba is 138.8<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwSg\">&nbsp;</span>km, among which 100<span typeof=\"mw:Entity\" id=\"mwSw\">&nbsp;</span>km are electrified.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"6\" id=\"mwTA\" aria-labelledby=\"History\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"History\">History</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6\" title=\"Edit section: History\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwTQ\">The province was created from <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guelma_Province\" title=\"Guelma Province\" id=\"mwTg\">Guelma Province</a> in 1984.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"7\" id=\"mwTw\" aria-labelledby=\"Administrative_divisions\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Administrative_divisions\">Administrative divisions</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwUA\">The province is divided into 10 <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/District\" title=\"District\" id=\"mwUQ\">districts</a> (<i id=\"mwUg\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daïra\" title=\"Daïra\" id=\"mwUw\">daïras</a></i>), which are further divided into 26 <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communes_of_Algeria\" title=\"Communes of Algeria\" id=\"mwVA\"><i id=\"mwVQ\">communes</i></a> or municipalities.</p>\n\n<section aria-labelledby=\"Further_reading\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Further_reading\">Further reading</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Souk_Ahras_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10\" title=\"Edit section: Further reading\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<ul id=\"mwYQ\"><li id=\"mwYg\"><style data-mw-deduplicate=\"TemplateStyles:r1333433106\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion\" about=\"#mwt19\" id=\"mwYw\"
